});\n    canvas.addEventListener('click', e => this._onClick(e));\n    this._buildNodes();\n    this._animate();\n    // Check health\n    this._checkNodes();\n    setInterval(() => this._checkNodes(), 10000);\n  },\n\n  resize() {\n    if (!this.canvas) return;\n    const r = this.canvas.parentElement.getBoundingClientRect();","numLines":100,"startLine":4500,"totalLines":4984}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01UgUKkys1g7FHHGyWXBPrKL","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"\n2\tREX — Proximity Unlock Daemon\n3\t================================\n4\tPhone unlocked + nearby → Mac stays unlocked.\n5\tPhone locks → Mac locks 60 seconds later.\n6\tYou never manually trigger anything.\n7\t\n8\tHow it works:\n9\t  1. This daemon runs silently in the background on your Mac Mini.\n10\t  2. The REX iPhone app (built in rex_heartbeat_app/) sends a UDP heartbeat\n11\t     every 8 seconds while your phone screen is ON and Face ID has passed.\n12\t  3. When your phone screen goes off (locks), iOS suspends the app.\n13\t     Heartbeats stop within 10 seconds.\n14\t  4. Daemon notices silence → starts countdown → locks Mac after LOCK_DELAY.\n15\t  5. When you pick up your phone → Face ID passes → app wakes → heartbeats resume\n16\t     → daemon detects → unlocks Mac screen automatically.\n17\t\n18\tThe result:\n19\t  • Sit at your Mac with phone nearby and unlocked → Mac is always open\n20\t  • Set phone face-down / screen dims while you keep typing → Mac stays open\n21\t    (Mac activity is checked — no lock while keyboard/mouse is in use)\n22\t  • Walk away and stop using Mac → Mac locks itself after 60s phone silence\n23\t    + 600s keyboard/mouse idle (both conditions must be true)\n24\t  • Come back, glance at phone (Face ID) → Mac unlocks before you sit down\n25\t  • Leave WiFi entirely → Mac locks within 60 seconds (secondary detection)\n26\t\n27\tLock conditions (BOTH must be true):\n28\t  • Phone heartbeats absent for LOCK_DELAY (60 seconds)\n29\t  • Mac keyboard/mouse idle for MAC_IDLE_THRESHOLD (600 seconds / 10 minutes)\n30\t\n31\tThis means: if you're actively working, your Mac will NEVER lock just because\n32\tyour phone screen went dark. It only locks when you've actually walked away.\n33\t\n34\tSecurity:\n35\t  • Heartbeats are HMAC-SHA256 signed — cannot be spoofed\n36\t  • Only accepted from same WiFi subnet\n37\t  • 30-second token window prevents replay\n38\t  • Face ID must pass on iPhone for heartbeats to start\n39\t  • All lock/unlock events logged with timestamp + IP\n40\t\n41\tStart automatically with Mac:\n42\t  python rex_proximity_daemon.py --install-launchagent\n43\t\n44\tManual run (for testing):\n45\t  python rex_proximity_daemon.py --run\n46\t\n47\tCheck status from another terminal:\n48\t  python rex_proximity_daemon.py --status\n49\t\"\"\"\n50\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"19a4a866-c15b-423b-9412-fae1a8f10f08","uuid":"fd50724c-c830-41e3-a999-eb960c7f4a9e","timestamp":"2026-06-07T22:14:13.036Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/mainsobhelper/Desktop/REX/rex_proximity_daemon.py","content":"\"\"\"\nREX — Proximity Unlock Daemon\n================================\nPhone unlocked + nearby → Mac stays unlocked.\nPhone locks → Mac locks 60 seconds later.\nYou never manually trigger anything.\n\nHow it works:\n  1. This daemon runs silently in the background on your Mac Mini.\n  2. The REX iPhone app (built in rex_heartbeat_app/) sends a UDP heartbeat\n     every 8 seconds while your phone screen is ON and Face ID has passed.\n  3. When your phone screen goes off (locks), iOS suspends the app.\n     Heartbeats stop within 10 seconds.\n  4. Daemon notices silence → starts countdown → locks Mac after LOCK_DELAY.\n  5. When you pick up your phone → Face ID passes → app wakes → heartbeats resume\n     → daemon detects → unlocks Mac screen automatically.\n\nThe result:\n  • Sit at your Mac with phone nearby and unlocked → Mac is always open\n  • Set phone face-down / screen dims while you keep typing → Mac stays open\n    (Mac activity is checked — no lock while keyboard/mouse is in use)\n  • Walk away and stop using Mac → Mac locks itself after 60s phone silence\n    + 600s keyboard/mouse idle (both conditions must be true)\n  • Come back, glance at phone (Face ID) → Mac unlocks before you sit down\n  • Leave WiFi entirely → Mac locks within 60 seconds (secondary detection)\n\nLock conditions (BOTH must be true):\n  • Phone heartbeats absent for LOCK_DELAY (60 seconds)\n  • Mac keyboard/mouse idle for MAC_IDLE_THRESHOLD (600 seconds / 10 minutes)\n\nThis means: if you're actively working, your Mac will NEVER lock just because\nyour phone screen went dark. It only locks when you've actually walked away.\n\nSecurity:\n  • Heartbeats are HMAC-SHA256 signed — cannot be spoofed\n  • Only accepted from same WiFi subnet\n  • 30-second token window prevents replay\n  • Face ID must pass on iPhone for heartbeats to start\n  • All lock/unlock events logged with timestamp + IP\n\nStart automatically with Mac:\n  python rex_proximity_daemon.py --install-launchagent\n\nManual run (for testing):\n  python rex_proximity_daemon.py --run\n\nCheck status from another terminal:\n  python rex_proximity_daemon.py --status\n\"\"\"\n","numLines":50,"startLine":1,"totalLines":621}}}

}\n11\twarn(){ echo -e \"${YELLOW}⚠️   $1${NC}\"; }\n12\t\n13\techo -e \"${BOLD}=== GHS TIGER CLAW SCREENSAVER SETUP ===${NC}\"\n14\techo \"Target: hermestigerclaw.com/cc as macOS screensaver / lock screen\"\n15\techo \"\"\n16\t\n17\t# Check macOS version\n18\tMACOS=$(sw_vers -productVersion)\n19\tMAJOR=$(echo \"$MACOS\" | cut -d. -f1)\n20\tinfo \"macOS version: $MACOS\"\n21\t\n22\t# Method 1: Try Web View Saver (macOS Sonoma+)\n23\tWEB_SAVER=\"/System/Library/Screen Savers/Web View Saver.saver\"\n24\tif [ -f \"$WEB_SAVER\" ]; then\n25\t    pass \"Web View Saver found at $WEB_SAVER\"\n26\t\n27\t    defaults -currentHost write com.apple.screensaver \\\n28\t        moduleDict -dict \\\n29\t        moduleName \"Web View Saver\" \\\n30\t        path \"$WEB_SAVER\" \\\n31\t        type 0\n32\t\n33\t    defaults -currentHost write com.apple.screensaver \\\n34\t        idleTime 300\n35\t\n36\t    # Set the URL for Web View Saver\n37\t    defaults -currentHost write com.apple.screensaverWebViewSaver URL \"https://hermestigerclaw.com/cc\"\n38\t\n39\t    pass \"Screensaver set: Web View Saver → hermestigerclaw.com/cc\"\n40\t    pass \"Idle time: 5 minutes\"\n41\t\n42\t    # Kill ScreenSaverEngine to force reload\n43\t    killall ScreenSaverEngine 2>/dev/null || true\n44\t\n45\telse\n46\t    warn \"Web View Saver not found — using iframe approach\"\n47\t\n48\t    # Method 2: Create a local screensaver wrapper HTML served by stats API\n49\t    info \"Command Center screensaver is built into CC_command_center.html\"\n50\t    info \"It activates after ~5 minutes of idle in the browser\"\n51\tfi\n52\t\n53\t# Check if Command Center is accessible\n54\tCC_LOCAL=$(curl -s --max-time 5 http://localhost:8001/cc 2>/dev/null | head -c 100)\n55\tif echo \"$CC_LOCAL\" | grep -qi \"GHS\\|command\\|hermes\"; then\n56\t    pass \"http://localhost:8001/cc is responding ✔\"\n57\telse\n58\t    warn \"localhost:8001/cc not responding — stats API may need restart\"\n59\t    info \"Run CC_fix_stats_api_final.command if needed\"\n60\tfi\n61\t\n62\techo \"\"\n63\techo -e \"${BOLD}=== MANUAL STEPS IF AUTO-SETUP DIDN'T WORK ===${NC}\"\n64\techo \"\"\n65\techo \"1. Open: System Settings → Screen Saver\"\n66\techo \"2. Select 'Web View Saver' (or 'Aerial' with URL support)\"\n67\techo \"3. Set URL to: https://hermestigerclaw.com/cc\"\n68\techo \"4. RED='\\033[0;31m'; CYAN='\\033[0;36m'; BOLD='\\033[1m'; YELLOW='\\033[1;33m'; NC='\\033[0m'\npass(){ echo -e \"${GREEN}✅  $1${NC}\"; }\nfail(){ echo -e \"${RED}❌  $1${NC}\"; }\ninfo(){ echo -e \"${CYAN}ℹ️   $1${NC}\"; }\nwarn(){ echo -e \"${YELLOW}⚠️   $1${NC}\"; }\n\necho -e \"${BOLD}=== GHS TIGER CLAW SCREENSAVER SETUP ===${NC}\"\necho \"Target: hermestigerclaw.com/cc as macOS screensaver / lock screen\"\necho \"\"\n\n# Check macOS version\nMACOS=$(sw_vers -productVersion)\nMAJOR=$(echo \"$MACOS\" | cut -d. -f1)\ninfo \"macOS version: $MACOS\"\n\n# Method 1: Try Web View Saver (macOS Sonoma+)\nWEB_SAVER=\"/System/Library/Screen Savers/Web View Saver.saver\"\nif [ -f \"$WEB_SAVER\" ]; then\n    pass \"Web View Saver found at $WEB_SAVER\"\n\n    defaults -currentHost write com.apple.screensaver \\\n        moduleDict -dict \\\n        moduleName \"Web View Saver\" \\\n        path \"$WEB_SAVER\" \\\n        type 0\n\n    defaults -currentHost write com.apple.screensaver \\\n        idleTime 300\n\n    # Set the URL for Web View Saver\n    defaults -currentHost write com.apple.screensaverWebViewSaver URL \"https://hermestigerclaw.com/cc\"\n\n    pass \"Screensaver set: Web View Saver → hermestigerclaw.com/cc\"\n    pass \"Idle time: 5 minutes\"\n\n    # Kill ScreenSaverEngine to force reload\n    killall ScreenSaverEngine 2>/dev/null || true\n\nelse\n    warn \"Web View Saver not found — using iframe approach\"\n\n    # Method 2: Create a local screensaver wrapper HTML served by stats API\n    info \"Command Center screensaver is built into CC_command_center.html\"\n    info \"It activates after ~5 minutes of idle in the browser\"\nfi\n\n# Check if Command Center is accessible\nCC_LOCAL=$(curl -s --max-time 5 http://localhost:8001/cc 2>/dev/null | head -c 100)\nif echo \"$CC_LOCAL\" | grep -qi \"GHS\\|command\\|hermes\"; Confirms TigerClaw :27226 is up\n#   2. Finds any Jarvis-related plist in ~/Library/LaunchAgents\n#   3. Reports what it found (does NOT load plist without confirmation)\n#   4. Provides exact load command for Kato to run after PAE-5 approval\n\nTIMESTAMP=$(date +\"%Y%m%d_%H%M%S\")\nLOG=~/Desktop/REX/logs/cc_jarvis_startup_${TIMESTAMP}.log\nmkdir -p ~/Desktop/REX/logs\nexec > >(tee \"$LOG\") 2>&1\n\necho \"══════════════════════════════════════════════════════\"\necho \"  CC_jarvis_startup — Phase 19 Jarvis HUD\"\necho \"  $(date)\"\necho \"  PAE-5 required before activating production\"\necho \"══════════════════════════════════════════════════════\"\necho \"\"\n\n# ── Step 1: Check TigerClaw API ───────────────────────────\necho \"── Step 1: TigerClaw API at :27226 ──────────────────\"\nTC_RESPONSE=$(curl -s --connect-timeout 3 http://localhost:27226/health 2>&1)\nTC_STATUS=$?\nif [ $TC_STATUS -eq 0 ] && [ -n \"$TC_RESPONSE\" ]; then\n  echo \"  ✅ TigerClaw :27226 RESPONDING\"\n  echo \"  Response: $TC_RESPONSE\"\nelse\n  echo \"  ❌ TigerClaw :27226 NOT RESPONDING\"\n  echo \"  Curl exit code: $TC_STATUS\"\n  echo \"\"\n  echo \"  → Look for TigerClaw plist/process:\"\n  TIGER_PLIST=$(ls ~/Library/LaunchAgents/ 2>/dev/null | grep -iE \"tiger\" | head -5)\n  if [ -n \"$TIGER_PLIST\" ]; then\n    echo \"  Found plists: $TIGER_PLIST\"\n    echo \"  Run: launchctl load ~/Library/LaunchAgents/$TIGER_PLIST\"\n  else\n    echo \"  No TigerClaw plist found in ~/Library/LaunchAgents/\"\n    echo \"  Check: ps aux | grep -i tiger\"\n    TIGER_PROC=$(ps aux | grep -i tiger | grep -v grep)\n    if [ -n \"$TIGER_PROC\" ]; then\n      echo \"  Tiger process(es) found:\"\n      echo \"  $TIGER_PROC\"\n    fi\n  fi\nfi\necho \"\"","numLines":50,"startLine":1,"totalLines":122}}}
